Looking at the legal moves, e4e1 is a very strong move because it's a check and forces the king to move.
In a professional game, checking the king and limiting its movement is usually the best strategy when you have a material advantage and are looking for mate.

Let's check for any tactical blunders.
Is there any way for white to counter e4e1+?
The only move is Kb2.
After Kb2, Black has several options.
One is a4a3+, which forces Kxa3.
Then Re3+ forces the king further.
This seems like a strong series of forcing moves.
